if the user is entering a negative coordinate\n        if (inputText.length() == 1 && inputText.charAt(0) == \'-\')\n            return -1;\n\n        try {\n            inputText = Since manually typed coordinates only moved the map when the "sync"/refresh button was pressed, typing coordinates and saving directly discarded the typed values and saved the map\'s center instead.\n\n## Changes\n\n**`app/src/main/java/com/cohenadair/anglerslog/locations/ManageFishingSpotFragment.java`**\n\n1. `verifyUserInput()` now reads the coordinates from the latitude/longitude text views via a new `getInputCoordinates()` helper, instead of from the map camera. If the coordinates can\'t be parsed or are out of range (lat ∉ [-90, 90], lng ∉ [-180, 180]), an error dialog is shown (`AlertUtils.showError`) and verification fails, so the save is blocked. Name checks were moved ahead of coordinate checks so the most relevant error appears first.\n2. Added helpers:\n   - `getInputCoordinates()` – parses and range-validates both text views, returning a `LatLng` or `null`.\n   - `parseCoordinateInput(InputTextView)` – parses text to a `Double` (handles `,` as decimal separator), returning `null` on invalid/partial input. Now parses as `double` rather than `float`, preserving the full precision of the 6-decimal coordinates.\n   - `isValidCoordinates(LatLng)` – range check that doesn\'t depend on the map fragment being available.\n   - `isPartialCoordinateInput(InputTextView)` – recognizes in-progress input (`-`, `.`, `-.`) so the user isn\'t nagged while typing.\n3. `validateCoordinateInput()` now returns a nullable `Double` instead of using `-1` as a sentinel (which was itself a legal coordinate) and still shows the existing toast + backspace behavior for genuinely bad input.\n4. The refresh button handler was updated for the new return type and now shows the error dialog when coordinates are out of range instead of silently doing nothing.\n\n**`app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml`** – added `error_fishing_spot_coordinates` for the invalid-coordinate dialog.\n\nExisting behavior is preserved: the text views stay in sync with map drags and camera updates, so simply moving the map and saving works exactly as before (the views hold the map center), and the Google Play Services-unavailable fallback still yields `0.0, 0.0`.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 823, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 32170, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 823, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfYLCoeRMW9nAsi1PXyhb', stop_reason=None, session_id='aac00b59-d314-4182-ba73-c3441ec66fbf', uuid='95947e0d-1059-4e3b-a65e-26d3dc81575f')
